Language: fra
Source: archive.org item lettresdifiant_v22jesu, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-10 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (627,938) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1781, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: Volume 22 of the collection, of the China mission: the Jesuits' own letters from China. The first letter is dated at Peking, 10 October 1731, at about OCR line 98.
